### User — 2026-09-10 03:39 UTC

I want a github repo for this to upload all my codes in this and all the three members should have access to it, so tell me how to do this and then tell me how to upload that to repo and how others will access the same

### Assistant — 2026-09-10 03:39 UTC

Absolutely. For your **GoMigo project with 3 members**, the best setup is:

**One GitHub repository → all 3 members added as collaborators → everyone works on their own local copy → changes are pushed/pulled through Git.**

I'll walk you through it from zero.

---

# 1. Create the GitHub repository

Go to urlGitHubhttps://github.com/ and log in.

### Step 1
Click the **+** button in the top-right → **New repository**.

### Step 2
Fill it like this:

**Repository name:**
```text
GoMigo
```

or something more descriptive:

```text
GoMigo-Travel-Companion
```

**Description:**
```text
GoMigo - Travel Companion Website
```

### Step 3 — Choose visibility

You have two choices:

- **Public** → anyone can see the code.
- **Private** → only you and the people you add can access it.

For a college project, I'd recommend **Private** unless your teacher specifically wants the code publicly visible.

### Step 4

If you're uploading an existing project, **don't initialize it with README, .gitignore, or license yet**. GitHub specifically recommends leaving these unchecked when importing an existing local project to avoid unnecessary merge conflicts. citeturn0search1turn0search5

Click:

**Create repository**

---

# 2. Add the other 2 members

Now you need to give your teammates access.

Inside your new repository:

**Settings → Collaborators → Add people**

Depending on GitHub's current interface, you may see something like:

**Settings → Collaborators → Manage access → Add people**

Enter each teammate's **GitHub username or email**.

For example:

```text
Member 1 → yourusername
Member 2 → teammate1
Member 3 → teammate2
```

Send the invitations.

They need to **accept the invitation**.

Once they accept it, all three of you can work on the same repository.

---

# 3. What your repository should eventually look like

For your GoMigo website, I'd recommend something like:

```text
GoMigo/
│
├── index.html
├── planning.html
├── profile.html
│
├── css/
│   ├── style.css
│   ├── planning.css
│   └── profile.css
│
├── js/
│   └── script.js
│
├── images/
│   ├── logo.png
│   ├── hero.png
│   ├── planning/
│   └── profile/
│
├── README.md
│
└── .gitignore
```

This will be much easier for your three members to manage than everyone uploading random files into the root folder.

---

# 4. Now upload your existing code

There are **two ways**.

For your project, I strongly recommend using **Git + VS Code/Terminal**, because then updating the project later becomes very easy.

## First: put all your GoMigo files into ONE folder

For example:

```text
Documents
   ↓
GoMigo
   ↓
index.html
planning.html
profile.html
css
images
js
```

Make sure your HTML/CSS/images are all inside this project folder.

---

# 5. Open that folder in VS Code

If you're using VS Code:

**File → Open Folder → GoMigo**

Then open:

**Terminal → New Terminal**

You'll see something similar to:

```text
C:\Users\YourName\Documents\GoMigo>
```

---

# 6. Initialize Git

Run:

```bash
git init
```

Then:

```bash
git add .
```

This tells Git to track all the files in your GoMigo folder. GitHub's documentation uses the same `git add .` → commit → push workflow for uploading local code. citeturn0search4

Then:

```bash
git commit -m "Initial GoMigo project"
```

---

# 7. Connect your folder to GitHub

Go back to your GitHub repository.

You'll have a URL similar to:

```text
https://github.com/YOUR-USERNAME/GoMigo.git
```

Copy that URL.

Then in your VS Code terminal:

```bash
git remote add origin https://github.com/YOUR-USERNAME/GoMigo.git
```

For example, if your username were `rishi123`:

```bash
git remote add origin https://github.com/rishi123/GoMigo.git
```

GitHub documents `git remote add origin ...` as the way to connect your local repository to the GitHub repository. citeturn0search9

---

# 8. Upload everything

Run:

```bash
git branch -M main
```

Then:

```bash
git push -u origin main
```

That's it.

Refresh your GitHub repository page.

You should now see:

```text
GoMigo

├── index.html
├── planning.html
├── profile.html
├── css/
├── images/
└── js/
```

🎉 **Your code is now on GitHub.**

---

# 9. How your teammates get the project

This is the important part.

Send them your repository link, something like:

```text
https://github.com/YOUR-USERNAME/GoMigo
```

They should first accept the collaborator invitation.

Then they can download the entire project by **cloning** it.

In their VS Code terminal:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/YOUR-USERNAME/GoMigo.git
```

This creates:

```text
GoMigo/
```

on their computer with the entire project.

GitHub also supports cloning through GitHub CLI with `gh repo clone OWNER/REPO`. citeturn0search7

---

# 10. How all 3 people work on the same project

This is where Git becomes really useful.

Imagine:

### You

You work on:

```text
planning.html
```

After finishing:

```bash
git add .
git commit -m "Updated planning page"
git push
```

Your changes are now on GitHub.

---

### Teammate 1

They work on:

```text
profile.html
```

Before starting work, they should run:

```bash
git pull
```

Then work on their files.

After finishing:

```bash
git add .
git commit -m "Updated profile page"
git push
```

---

### Teammate 2

They can work on:

```text
index.html
```

or CSS/images/etc.

Same process:

```bash
git pull
```

Work.

Then:

```bash
git add .
git commit -m "Updated homepage"
git push
```

---

# 11. The most important 3 commands

Your team should remember these:

### Before you start working

```bash
git pull
```

### After making changes

```bash
git add .
git commit -m "Describe what you changed"
```

### Upload your changes

```bash
git push
```

So your basic workflow becomes:

```text
        GitHub
          ↑
        git push
          ↑
       Your PC
          ↓
       git pull
          ↓
        GitHub
```

All three members are connected to the **same repository**.

---

# 12. One VERY important rule for your team

Don't have all three people editing the **same file at the same time** if you can avoid it.

For example:

| Member | Work |
|---|---|
| Member 1 | Homepage |
| Member 2 | Planning page |
| Member 3 | Profile page |

Then later you can combine everything.

For GoMigo, you could organize it like:

```text
Member 1
→ Homepage
→ Navbar
→ Footer

Member 2
→ Trip Planning
→ Destination pages

Member 3
→ Profile
→ Forms
→ UI components
```

This greatly reduces Git conflicts.

---

# 13. What if two people edit the same file?

Git may give you a **merge conflict**.

For example:

```text
<<<<<<< HEAD
your code
=======
teammate's code
>>>>>>> main
```

Don't panic.

It simply means Git doesn't know which version should remain.

You manually choose/combine the correct code, save the file, then:

```bash
git add .
git commit -m "Resolved merge conflict"
git push
```

---

# 14. Also add a `.gitignore`

For a web project, create:

```text
.gitignore
```

You can put things like:

```text
.vscode/
.DS_Store
Thumbs.db
node_modules/
```

**Most importantly:** never upload passwords, API keys, secret tokens, or private credentials to GitHub.

---

# 15. Your final GoMigo workflow

I'd recommend your team follows this exact routine:

```text
                 ┌─────────────────┐
                 │     GitHub      │
                 │ GoMigo Repository│
                 └────────┬────────┘
                          │
             ┌────────────┼────────────┐
             ↓            ↓            ↓
          Member 1     Member 2     Member 3
             │            │            │
          Homepage     Planning      Profile
             │            │            │
             └────── git push ────────┘
                          │
                       GitHub
```

### Every morning / before coding:

```bash
git pull
```

### After finishing your work:

```bash
git add .
git commit -m "What I changed"
git push
```

### If someone else pushed changes:

```bash
git pull
```

---

## ⭐ What I recommend you do right now

Don't worry about all the Git commands at once.

Do these **in order**:

**1. Create the GitHub repository**

**2. Add the other two members as collaborators**

**3. Put your GoMigo HTML/CSS/JS/images into one `GoMigo` folder**

**4. Open that folder in VS Code**

**5. Open Terminal**

**6. Run:**

```bash
git init
git add .
git commit -m "Initial GoMigo project"
```

**7. Connect it:**

```bash
git remote add origin YOUR-GITHUB-REPOSITORY-URL
```

**8. Upload:**

```bash
git branch -M main
git push -u origin main
```

Then **send your teammates the repository link**. They clone it, and from that point onward all three of you can work on the same project.

If you want, I can also guide you **click-by-click through creating the GitHub repo and adding the 2 teammates first**, and then we'll do the terminal commands one at a time so you don't get any errors. citeturn0search1turn0search5

### User — 2026-09-10 04:14 UTC

while entering remote origin i made a mistake so again in next command i tried correct one but now its not adding and saying remote origin already exists what to do now

### Assistant — 2026-09-10 04:14 UTC

No problem — this is a very common Git situation. 😊

You already created a remote named `origin`, so Git won't let you create another one with the same name.

### Do this

First, check what URL is currently saved:

```bash
git remote -v
```

You'll probably see your **wrong GitHub URL**.

Then replace it with the correct one:

```bash
git remote set-url origin https://github.com/YOUR-USERNAME/GoMigo.git
```

Replace the URL with your actual repository URL.

Then verify:

```bash
git remote -v
```

It should now show the **correct repository** for both `fetch` and `push`.

### Then continue with:

```bash
git branch -M main
git push -u origin main
```

That's all—you **do not need to run `git remote add origin` again**.

If you paste here what you get from:

```bash
git remote -v
```

(Ignore/hide anything sensitive if present), I can tell you exactly what command to run next.

- width/height
- padding
- margins
- colors
- fonts
- font sizes
- border radius
- shadows
- spacing

You can then give those specifications to me and I can turn them into clean HTML/CSS.

This usually produces **better code** than one-click converters.

- **urlFigma Dev Modehttps://www.figma.com/dev-mode/** — inspect designs and developer specifications

These can give you a starting point, but **don't submit their output without checking it**. AI converters often produce excessive `<div>` elements, awkward positioning, unnecessary absolute coordinates, and CSS that is difficult to maintain.

# What about Claude, Cursor, Lovable, etc.?

This is actually a good question.

For your particular assignment, I'd rank the approaches roughly like this:

### 🥇 Figma + ChatGPT

**Best if you want control and want to understand the code.**

You give me the designs → I create the structure → you run it → we compare it with Figma → I fix discrepancies.

This is especially good if your sir might ask:

> "How did you implement this?"

because you'll have understandable HTML/CSS rather than an enormous AI-generated project.

---

### 🥈 Claude

**Very good for large codebases.**

Claude is particularly useful once you have hundreds/thousands of lines of code and want an AI to understand the entire project.

For example:

> "Here are my 5 pages. Refactor the CSS so they share a common navigation component."

That's a good Claude use case.

But it doesn't magically solve the fundamental problem of accurately interpreting your Figma design.

---

### 🥉 Cursor

**Excellent once you have the project locally.**

Cursor can work directly with:

```text
index.html
planning.html
style.css
script.js
assets/
```

and modify the files while you work.

I'd actually recommend:

**Me → generate the initial implementation**

then

**Cursor → help you edit/debug the project locally.**

---

### Lovable / Bolt / similar AI website builders

These are great if your goal is:

> "Build me a website quickly."

But they're less ideal if your professor specifically wants:

> **HTML + CSS implementation of our Figma design.**

They may generate React/Tailwind/etc. and considerably more infrastructure than you need.

# 2. `index.html`

Create `index.html` and paste this:

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">

    <title>GoMigo — Travel Your Way</title>

    <link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css">
</head>

<body>

    <!-- =====================================================
         HERO SECTION
    ====================================================== -->

    <section class="hero">

        <nav class="navbar">

            <div class="nav-left">

                <div class="menu-icon">
                    <span></span>
                    <span></span>
                    <span></span>
                </div>

                <img src="assets/gomigo-logo.png"
                     class="logo"
                     alt="GoMigo">

            </div>

            <div class="nav-links">
                <a href="#explore">Explore</a>
                <a href="#destinations">Destinations</a>
                <a href="#how-it-works">Travel Guide</a>
                <a href="#features">My Trips</a>
            </div>

            <div class="nav-right">
                <a href="#">Login</a>

                <a href="planning.html" class="get-started">
                    Get Started
                </a>
            </div>

        </nav>


        <div class="hero-content">

            <h1>
                Go Beyond<br>
                Travel...
            </h1>

            <p>
                GoMigo brings your travel ideas together and<br>
                helps you create the perfect trip, your way..
            </p>

            <div class="hero-buttons">

                <a href="planning.html" class="hero-btn">
                    Plan Your Trip
                </a>

                <a href="#features" class="hero-btn">
                    Get Inspired
                </a>

            </div>

        </div>


        <a href="#how-it-works" class="learn-more">
            Learn More ↓
        </a>

    </section>


    <!-- =====================================================
         HOW IT WORKS
    ====================================================== -->

    <section class="how-it-works" id="how-it-works">

        <h2>How it Works?</h2>


        <div class="planned-section">

            <div class="planned-text">

                <h3>
                    Your Trip planned,<br>
                    Around you
                </h3>

                <p>
                    GoMigo takes the stress out of travel
                    planning by bringing your destination,
                    preferences, budget, and interests
                    together to create a trip that feels
                    uniquely yours.
                </p>

            </div>


            <div class="destination-collage">

                <img src="assets/taj-mahal.png"
                     class="taj"
                     alt="Taj Mahal">

                <img src="assets/eiffel-tower.png"
                     class="eiffel"
                     alt="Eiffel Tower">

                <img src="assets/lotus-temple.png"
                     class="lotus"
                     alt="Lotus Temple">

                <img src="assets/leaning-tower.png"
                     class="leaning"
                     alt="Leaning Tower of Pisa">

                <img src="assets/colosseum.png"
                     class="colosseum"
                     alt="Colosseum">

                <img src="assets/india-gate.png"
                     class="india-gate"
                     alt="India Gate">

            </div>

        </div>


        <!-- START WITH WHERE YOU WANT TO GO -->

        <div class="how-row">

            <div class="how-image">
                <img src="assets/trip-planning.png"
                     alt="Trip planning">
            </div>

            <div class="how-text">

                <h3>
                    Start with where you<br>
                    want to Go.
                </h3>

                <p>
                    Select a destination you’re excited to
                    explore, whether it’s a weekend getaway,
                    a long vacation, or somewhere completely
                    new. Choose a specific place, plan multiple
                    destinations, and add your travel dates
                    and trip duration.
                </p>

            </div>

        </div>


        <!-- PERSONALIZED RECOMMENDATIONS -->

        <div class="how-row reverse">

            <div class="how-text">

                <h3>
                    Get Personalized Recommendations
                </h3>

                <p>
                    Discover travel experiences tailored
                    just for you. Based on your interests
                    and preferences, GoMigo suggests
                    destinations, attractions, and
                    activities you’ll love.
                </p>

            </div>


            <div class="how-image">

                <img src="assets/recommendations.png"
                     alt="Personalized recommendations">

            </div>

        </div>


        <!-- PLAN WITH YOUR CREW -->

        <div class="how-row">

            <div class="how-image">

                <img src="assets/crew.png"
                     alt="Plan with your crew">

            </div>

            <div class="how-text">

                <h3>
                    Plan with your crew.
                </h3>

                <p>
                    Travel together, your way. Invite
                    friends and family, chat in one place,
                    share ideas, and build a trip plan
                    everyone will love — without the
                    endless group texts.
                </p>

            </div>

        </div>

    </section>


    <!-- =====================================================
         GOMIGO FEATURES
    ====================================================== -->

    <section class="features" id="features">

        <h2>GoMigo Features</h2>

        <h3>
            Everything you need to plan, explore and travel your way
        </h3>

            <img src="assets/explore-destinations.png"

            <img src="assets/personalized.png"
                 alt="Personalized Recommendations">

            </div>

            <div class="footer-column">

                <h3>
                    <span>for</span> Company
                </h3>

                <a href="#">About</a>
                <a href="#">Team</a>
                <a href="#">Press</a>
                <a href="#">Media Kit</a>
                <a href="#">Contact</a>
                <a href="#">Resources</a>
                <a href="#">Mindtrip App</a>

            </div>

        </div>


        <!-- SOCIAL MEDIA -->

        <div class="social-icons">

            <a href="#">
                <img src="assets/instagram.png"
                     alt="Instagram">
            </a>

            <a href="#">
                <img src="assets/facebook.png"
                     alt="Facebook">
            </a>

            <a href="#">
                <img src="assets/x.png"
                     alt="X">
            </a>

            <a href="#">
                <img src="assets/youtube.png"
                     alt="YouTube">
            </a>

            <a href="#">
                <img src="assets/linkedin.png"
                     alt="LinkedIn">
            </a>

        </div>

    </footer>

</body>

</html>
```

# 3. `style.css`

Now create `style.css` and paste this:

```css
/* =========================================================
   GOOGLE FONTS
========================================================= */

@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600&family=K2D:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Manrope:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap');


/* =========================================================
   GLOBAL
========================================================= */

* {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
}

html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
}

body {
    background: #EAF8F0;
    color: #000000;
    overflow-x: hidden;
}

a {
    text-decoration: none;
    color: inherit;
}

img {
    display: block;
    max-width: 100%;
}


/* =========================================================
   HERO
========================================================= */

.hero {
    width: calc(100% - 104px);
    max-width: 1440px;
    min-height: 900px;

    margin: 0 auto;

    position: relative;

    background-image: url("assets/hero-background.png");
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center;
    background-repeat: no-repeat;

    border-radius: 0 0 100px 100px;

    color: #EAF8F0;

    overflow: hidden;
}


/* =========================================================
   NAVBAR
========================================================= */

.navbar {
    width: 100%;

    padding: 25px 38px;

    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
}


.nav-left {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 22px;
}


.menu-icon {
    width: 37px;
    height: 28px;

    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;

    cursor: pointer;
}


.menu-icon span {
    width: 100%;
    height: 4px;

    background: #EAF8F0;

    border-radius: 10px;
}


.menu-icon span:nth-child(2) {
    width: 70%;
}


.logo {
    width: 100px;
    height: auto;
}


.nav-links {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 58px;

    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;
    font-size: 20px;
    font-weight: 600;
}


.nav-right {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 30px;

    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;
    font-size: 20px;
    font-weight: 600;
}


.get-started {
    padding: 15px 23px;

    border-radius: 35px;

    background: rgba(234, 248, 240, 0.27);
}


/* =========================================================
   HERO CONTENT
========================================================= */

.hero-content {
    position: absolute;

    left: 48px;
    top: 245px;
}


.hero-content h1 {
    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-size: 128px;
    line-height: 0.98;

    font-weight: 700;

    letter-spacing: -4px;
}


.hero-content p {
    margin-top: 38px;

    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-size: 26px;
    line-height: 1.55;

    font-weight: 500;
}


.hero-buttons {
    margin-top: 48px;

    display: flex;
    gap: 28px;
}


.hero-btn {
    padding: 16px 25px;

    border: 1.5px solid #EAF8F0;

    border-radius: 35px;

    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-size: 20px;
    font-weight: 600;

    transition: 0.2s ease;
}


.hero-btn:hover {
    background: #EAF8F0;
    color: #024C60;
}


.learn-more {
    position: absolute;

    bottom: 45px;
    left: 50%;

    transform: translateX(-50%);

    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-size: 20px;
    font-weight: 600;
}


/* =========================================================
   HOW IT WORKS
========================================================= */

.how-it-works {
    width: 100%;

    padding: 100px 7% 150px;

    background: #EAF8F0;
}


.how-it-works > h2 {
    text-align: center;

    font-family: 'Manrope', sans-serif;

    font-size: 84px;
    line-height: 1.1;

    font-weight: 700;

    margin-bottom: 150px;
}


/* =========================================================
   FIRST HOW IT WORKS SECTION
========================================================= */

.planned-section {
    width: 100%;

    min-height: 600px;

    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;

    gap: 70px;

    margin-bottom: 160px;
}


.planned-text {
    width: 42%;
}


.planned-text h3 {
    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-size: 36px;
    line-height: 1.35;

    font-weight: 700;
}


.planned-text p {
    margin-top: 22px;

    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-size: 32px;
    line-height: 1.4;

    font-weight: 400;

    color: #000000;
}


.destination-collage {
    position: relative;

    width: 52%;
    height: 600px;
}


.destination-collage img {
    position: absolute;

    object-fit: cover;

    border-radius: 22px;
}


/* Taj Mahal */

.destination-collage .taj {
    width: 230px;
    height: 250px;

    left: 5%;
    top: 80px;
}


/* Eiffel */

.destination-collage .eiffel {
    width: 190px;
    height: 250px;

    left: 38%;
    top: 0;
}


/* Lotus */

.destination-collage .lotus {
    width: 230px;
    height: 200px;

    right: 0;
    top: 80px;
}


/* Pisa */

.destination-collage .leaning {
    width: 190px;
    height: 220px;

    left: 18%;
    bottom: 10px;
}


/* Colosseum */

.destination-collage .colosseum {
    width: 200px;
    height: 190px;

    left: 49%;
    top: 250px;
}


/* India Gate */

.destination-collage .india-gate {
    width: 190px;
    height: 230px;

    right: 0;
    bottom: 0;
}


/* =========================================================
   HOW IT WORKS ROWS
========================================================= */

.how-row {
    width: 100%;

    min-height: 600px;

    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;

    gap: 90px;

    margin-bottom: 170px;
}


.how-row.reverse {
    flex-direction: row;
}


.how-image {
    width: 53%;

    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
}


.how-image img {
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 700px;
}


.how-text {
    width: 47%;
}


.how-text h3 {
    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-size: 36px;
    line-height: 1.35;

    font-weight: 700;
}


.how-text p {
    margin-top: 20px;

    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-size: 32px;
    line-height: 1.4;

    font-weight: 400;
}


/* =========================================================
   FEATURES
========================================================= */

.features {
    width: 100%;

    padding: 100px 7% 150px;

    background: #EAF8F0;
}


.features h2 {
    text-align: center;

    font-family: 'Manrope', sans-serif;

    font-size: 84px;

    font-weight: 700;
}


.features > h3 {
    margin-top: 22px;

    text-align: center;

    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-size: 36px;

    font-weight: 600;
}


.feature-cards {
    margin-top: 70px;

    display: grid;

    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);

    gap: 55px;

    align-items: start;
}


.feature-cards img {
    width: 100%;

    border-radius: 25px;
}


/* =========================================================
   ORGANIZE
========================================================= */

.organize {
    width: 100%;

    padding: 40px 7% 150px;

    background: #EAF8F0;
}


.organize h2 {
    text-align: center;

    font-family: 'Manrope', sans-serif;

    font-size: 60px;

    font-weight: 700;

    margin-bottom: 100px;
}


.organize-grid {
    display: grid;

    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);

    gap: 70px 55px;
}


.organize-grid img {
    width: 100%;

    border-radius: 25px;
}


/* =========================================================
   FOOTER
========================================================= */

.footer {
    width: 100%;

    min-height: 700px;

    padding: 100px 10% 60px;

    background: #EAF8F0;

    position: relative;
}


.footer-content {
    width: 55%;

    margin-left: auto;

    display: grid;

    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);

    gap: 70px;
}


.footer-column {
    display: flex;

    flex-direction: column;

    align-items: center;

    text-align: center;
}


.footer-column h3 {
    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-size: 26px;

    font-weight: 400;

    margin-bottom: 27px;
}


.footer-column h3 span {
    font-family: 'K2D', sans-serif;

    font-style: italic;

    font-weight: 400;
}


.footer-column a {
    font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;

    font-size: 14px;

    margin-bottom: 20px;
}


.business-heading {
    margin-top: 45px;
}


/* =========================================================
   SOCIAL ICONS
========================================================= */

.social-icons {
    position: absolute;

    right: 10%;
    bottom: 60px;

    display: flex;

    align-items: center;

    gap: 65px;
}


.social-icons img {
    width: 35px;
    height: 35px;

    object-fit: contain;
}


/* =========================================================
   RESPONSIVE
========================================================= */

@media (max-width: 1200px) {

    .nav-links {
        gap: 25px;
        font-size: 17px;
    }

    .nav-right {
        font-size: 17px;
    }

    .hero-content h1 {
        font-size: 90px;
    }

    .hero-content p {
        font-size: 22px;
    }

    .how-it-works > h2,
    .features h2 {
        font-size: 65px;
    }

    .planned-text p,
    .how-text p {
        font-size: 25px;
    }

}


@media (max-width: 900px) {

    .hero {
        width: 100%;
        border-radius: 0 0 60px 60px;
    }

    .nav-links {
        display: none;
    }

    .hero-content {
        left: 35px;
        top: 250px;
    }

    .hero-content h1 {
        font-size: 75px;
    }

    .hero-content p {
        font-size: 20px;
    }

    .planned-section,
    .how-row,
    .how-row.reverse {
        flex-direction: column;
    }

    .planned-text,
    .how-text,
    .how-image {
        width: 100%;
    }

    .destination-collage {
        width: 100%;
    }

    .feature-cards {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .organize-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .footer-content {
        width: 100%;
        margin: 0;

        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .social-icons {
        position: static;

        justify-content: center;

        margin-top: 60px;
    }

}


@media (max-width: 600px) {

    .navbar {
        padding: 20px;
    }

    .logo {
        width: 85px;
    }

    .get-started {
        padding: 11px 15px;
    }

    .hero-content {
        left: 25px;
        right: 25px;
    }

    .hero-content h1 {
        font-size: 60px;
        letter-spacing: -2px;
    }

    .hero-content p {
        font-size: 17px;
    }

    .hero-buttons {
        gap: 10px;
    }

    .hero-btn {
        padding: 12px 16px;
        font-size: 16px;
    }

    .how-it-works,
    .features,
    .organize {
        padding-left: 25px;
        padding-right: 25px;
    }

    .how-it-works > h2,
    .features h2 {
        font-size: 48px;
    }

    .features > h3 {
        font-size: 24px;
    }

    .organize h2 {
        font-size: 42px;
    }

    .planned-text p,
    .how-text p {
        font-size: 22px;
    }

}
```

# 4. How to run it in VS Code

This part is very easy.

### Step 1

Install **VS Code** if you haven't already.

Open VS Code.

Then choose:

**File → Open Folder**

and select:

```text
GoMigo
```

---

### Step 2

You should see:

```text
GoMigo
│
├── index.html
├── style.css
└── assets
```

in the left Explorer panel.

---

### Step 3

Put all your image assets inside:

```text
assets
```

For example:

```text
assets/
   hero-background.png
   gomigo-logo.png
   taj-mahal.png
```

Make sure the spelling matches the HTML.

For example, the HTML says:

```html
<img src="assets/taj-mahal.png">
```

so the actual file must be:

```text
taj-mahal.png
```

---

# 5. Open the website

The easiest way is to install the **Live Server** extension in VS Code.

In VS Code:

**Extensions** → search:

```text
Live Server
```

Install it.

Then open:

```text
index.html
```

Right-click inside the file and choose:

**Open with Live Server**

Your browser will open the GoMigo website.

---

# 6. Your navigation is already prepared

I've intentionally put:

```html
<a href="planning.html">
```

in the relevant places.

So when we create your second page, you'll simply have:

```text
GoMigo/
│
├── index.html
├── planning.html
├── profile.html
├── style.css
│
└── assets/
```

and:

**Home → Plan Your Trip → Trip Planning**

will work without JavaScript.

Then:

**Trip Planning → Profile**

can work the same way.

---

## ⚠️ One important point about the first version

I have **not tried to fake your Figma's image assets with CSS**.

For example, your three feature cards are already complete visual designs, so we're using:

```html
<img src="assets/ai-trip-planner.png">
```

rather than rebuilding the card from scratch.

Likewise, your six **Hotels / Car Rental / Flight Booking / Restaurants / Experiences / Tours** cards are complete assets, so we're using those directly.

That is actually the best approach for getting **very close to your Figma design**.

---

# What we should do next

Don't worry if the first browser version isn't **pixel-perfect** immediately.

Once you run this, take **one screenshot of the browser showing the Home page** and send it to me.

I'll then compare the implementation against the Figma screenshots you've already given me and we can adjust:

- hero height
- background position
- heading position
- image sizes
- section spacing
- card sizes
- footer position
- typography
- margins/padding

until it looks much closer to your Figma.

### And after Home is finalized:

We'll do exactly the same process for:

**2️⃣ Trip Planning**

then

**3️⃣ Profile**

and finally connect all three.

**For now, create the folder + `index.html` + `style.css`, put your assets into `assets/`, and run it with Live Server.**

Yes. Since your teammates added the images to the shared Figma file, **you can download those assets directly from Figma**. You don't need to ask them to send every image individually.

### Method 1 — Download an individual image ⭐

1. Open the **GoMigo Figma file**.
2. Go to the **Introduction** page.
3. Select the image you want.
4. Look at the **right-hand Design panel**.
5. Scroll down to **Export**.
6. Click the **`+`** beside Export if needed.
7. Choose **PNG**.
8. Click **Export [layer/image name]**.
9. Save it into your computer's GoMigo folder.

For example, select the hero background layer:

```text
Home-background 1
```

Then:

**Export → PNG → Export Home-background 1**

```text
│
```

```text
├── instagram.svg
├── facebook.svg
├── x.svg

```

SVG will generally remain sharper when resized.

Absolutely. Let's do **only the HTML first**. Don't worry about CSS yet.

Create a file called **`index.html`** inside your GoMigo folder and paste this entire code into it.

> I’m using the asset names we discussed. If your actual filenames are different, we can change them afterward.

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">

    <title>GoMigo — Travel Your Way</title>

    <!-- CSS file -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css">

    <!-- Google Fonts -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>

    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700&family=K2D:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Manrope:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
</head>

<body>

    <!-- =====================================================
         HERO SECTION
    ====================================================== -->

    <section class="hero">

        <!-- Navigation -->
        <nav class="navbar">

            <div class="nav-left">

                <!-- Menu icon -->
                <button class="menu-button" aria-label="Open menu">
                    <img src="assets/menu.svg" alt="Menu">
                </button>

                <!-- GoMigo Logo -->
                <a href="index.html" class="logo-link">
                    <img src="assets/gomigo-logo.png" alt="GoMigo Logo" class="logo">
                </a>

            </div>

            <!-- Navigation links -->
            <div class="nav-links">

                <a href="#explore">Explore</a>

                <a href="#destinations">Destinations</a>

                <a href="#travel-guide">Travel Guide</a>

                <a href="#my-trips">My Trips</a>

            </div>

            <!-- Login / Get Started -->
            <div class="nav-actions">
```

### What this HTML already does

I've structured it according to your Figma page:

**Home page**

```text
Hero
 ↓
How it Works?
 ↓
Your Trip planned, Around you
 ↓
Start with where you want to Go
 ↓
Get Personalized Recommendations
 ↓
Plan with your crew
 ↓
GoMigo Features
 ↓
Organize it all in one place
 ↓
Footer
```

And I've already prepared the navigation for your later pages:

```text
Get Started → trip-planning.html
Plan Your Trip → trip-planning.html
Logo → index.html
```

So when we create your second page, we won't have to rewrite the whole project.

### One important thing before we move to CSS

Some of the asset names I used are **examples**, because I don't yet know the exact filenames you gave the images when you downloaded them.

For example, I used:

```text
assets/taj-mahal.png
assets/ai-trip-planner.png
assets/hotels-icon.png
```

If your folder has names like:

```text
image1.png
image2.png
Hotels.png
Group.png
```

that's completely fine. **Don't rename everything yet.**

Once you've pasted this into `index.html`, send me a **screenshot of your `assets` folder in VS Code/File Explorer**. I'll map every image to the correct filename, and then we'll write the **CSS to reproduce your Figma layout as closely as possible**.

### Assistant — 2026-09-10 02:01 UTC

Yes. Now create **`style.css`** in the same folder as `index.html` and paste the following code.

This CSS is written specifically around the **GoMigo Figma Home page you shared**, including your fonts, sizes, colors, spacing, rounded cards, hero background, feature cards, and footer.

```css
/* =========================================================
   GOMIGO - GLOBAL STYLES
========================================================= */

* {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
}

html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
}

body {
    background: #EAF8F0;
    color: #000000;
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;
    overflow-x: hidden;
}

a {
    text-decoration: none;
    color: inherit;
}

img {
    display: block;
    max-width: 100%;
}


/* =========================================================
   HERO SECTION
========================================================= */

.hero {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
    min-height: 100vh;

    background-image: url("assets/hero-background.png");
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center top;
    background-repeat: no-repeat;

    border-radius: 0 0 100px 100px;

    overflow: hidden;
}


/* =========================================================
   NAVBAR
========================================================= */

.navbar {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;

    width: 100%;
    height: 100px;

    padding: 25px 60px;

    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;

    z-index: 10;

    color: #EAF8F0;
}


/* Left side of navbar */

.nav-left {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 25px;
}


/* Menu */

.menu-button {
    background: none;
    border: none;
    cursor: pointer;

    width: 42px;
    height: 42px;

    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
}

.menu-button img {
    width: 38px;
    height: 38px;
}


/* Logo */

.logo-link {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
}

.logo {
    width: 110px;
    height: auto;
}


/* Navigation links */

.nav-links {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;

    gap: 55px;

    margin-left: auto;
    margin-right: 70px;

    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;
    font-size: 20px;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.nav-links a {
    transition: opacity 0.2s ease;
}

.nav-links a:hover {
    opacity: 0.75;
}


/* Login and Get Started */

.nav-actions {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 45px;

    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;
    font-size: 20px;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.login-link {
    white-space: nowrap;
}

.get-started-button {
    padding: 15px 24px;

    background: rgba(234, 248, 240, 0.25);

    border-radius: 35px;

    white-space: nowrap;

    transition: background 0.2s ease;
}

.get-started-button:hover {
    background: rgba(234, 248, 240, 0.4);
}


/* =========================================================
   HERO CONTENT
========================================================= */

.hero-content {
    position: relative;

    min-height: 100vh;

    display: flex;
    align-items: center;

    padding-left: 7%;
    padding-top: 40px;

    z-index: 2;
}


.hero-text {
    color: #EAF8F0;
    margin-top: 25px;
}


/* Main hero heading */

.hero-text h1 {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 128px;
    line-height: 1.02;

    font-weight: 700;

    letter-spacing: -4px;

    margin-bottom: 30px;
}


/* Hero description */

.hero-text p {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 26px;
    line-height: 1.55;

    font-weight: 500;

    margin-bottom: 50px;
}


/* Hero buttons */

.hero-buttons {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 30px;
}

.hero-button {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 20px;
    font-weight: 600;

    color: #EAF8F0;

    border: 1.5px solid #EAF8F0;

    border-radius: 35px;

    padding: 15px 25px;

    transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

.hero-button:hover {
    background: #EAF8F0;
    color: #024C60;
}


/* =========================================================
   LEARN MORE
========================================================= */

.learn-more {
    position: absolute;

    bottom: 35px;
    left: 50%;

    transform: translateX(-50%);

    z-index: 5;

    color: #EAF8F0;

    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 20px;
    font-weight: 600;
}


/* =========================================================
   HOW IT WORKS
========================================================= */

.how-it-works {
    width: 100%;

    background: #EAF8F0;

    padding: 90px 7% 120px;
}


/* Main heading */

.how-it-works > h2 {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 84px;
    line-height: 1.1;

    font-weight: 700;

    text-align: center;

    margin-bottom: 130px;
}


/* =========================================================
   GENERAL HOW-IT-WORKS ROW
========================================================= */

.how-section {
    width: 100%;

    min-height: 500px;

    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;

    gap: 80px;

    margin-bottom: 140px;
}


/* Text */

.how-text {
    flex: 1;
    max-width: 620px;
}

.how-text h3 {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 36px;
    line-height: 1.3;

    font-weight: 700;

    margin-bottom: 18px;
}

.how-text p {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 32px;
    line-height: 1.45;

    font-weight: 400;

    color: #000000;
}


/* =========================================================
   DESTINATION COLLAGE
========================================================= */

.trip-planned {
    align-items: center;
}


/* Left text */

.trip-planned .how-text {
    padding-left: 0;
}


/* Destination images */

.destination-collage {
    position: relative;

    width: 650px;
    height: 520px;

    flex-shrink: 0;
}


/* All six destination images */

.destination-collage img {
    position: absolute;

    object-fit: cover;

    border-radius: 20px;
}


/* Taj Mahal */

.destination-collage img:nth-child(1) {
    width: 230px;
    height: 250px;

    left: 30px;
    top: 80px;
}


/* Eiffel Tower */

.destination-collage img:nth-child(2) {
    width: 195px;
    height: 250px;

    left: 280px;
    top: 0;
}


/* Lotus Temple */

.destination-collage img:nth-child(3) {
    width: 205px;
    height: 200px;

    right: 0;
    top: 70px;
}


/* Leaning Tower */

.destination-collage img:nth-child(4) {
    width: 185px;
    height: 220px;

    left: 120px;
    bottom: 0;
}


/* Colosseum */

.destination-collage img:nth-child(5) {
    width: 195px;
    height: 190px;

    left: 335px;
    bottom: 60px;
}


/* India Gate */

.destination-collage img:nth-child(6) {
    width: 190px;
    height: 240px;

    right: 5px;
    bottom: -5px;
}


/* =========================================================
   HOW IT WORKS IMAGE SECTIONS
========================================================= */

.how-image {
    flex: 1;

    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
}

.how-image img {
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 680px;

    height: auto;

    object-fit: contain;
}


/* First image on left */

.destination-section .how-image {
    justify-content: flex-start;
}


/* Personalized image on right */

.recommendations-section .how-image {
    justify-content: flex-end;
}


/* Crew image on left */

.crew-section .how-image {
    justify-content: flex-start;
}


/* =========================================================
   GOMIGO FEATURES
========================================================= */

.features {
    width: 100%;

    background: #EAF8F0;

    padding: 30px 7% 120px;
}


/* Features heading */

.section-heading {
    text-align: center;

    margin-bottom: 75px;
}

.section-heading h2 {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 84px;
    line-height: 1.1;

    font-weight: 700;

    margin-bottom: 20px;
}

.section-heading p {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 36px;
    font-weight: 600;

    line-height: 1.3;
}


/* Feature cards container */

.feature-cards {
    width: 100%;

    display: grid;

    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);

    gap: 45px;
}


/* Individual feature card */

.feature-card {
    overflow: hidden;

    border-radius: 25px;

    background: transparent;
}


/* Feature graphic */

.feature-card > img {
    width: 100%;
    height: auto;

    border-radius: 25px;

    margin-bottom: 25px;
}


/* Feature title */

.feature-card h3 {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 30px;
    line-height: 1.2;

    font-weight: 700;

    margin-bottom: 8px;
}


/* Colored subtitle */

.feature-card h4 {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 25px;
    line-height: 1.3;

    font-weight: 700;

    margin-bottom: 12px;
}


/* Description */

.feature-card p {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 20px;
    line-height: 1.45;

    color: #60758A;
}


/* =========================================================
   FEATURE CARD INDIVIDUAL COLORS
========================================================= */

.feature-card:nth-child(1) h4 {
    color: #0874D1;
}

.feature-card:nth-child(2) h4 {
    color: #6A46E8;
}

.feature-card:nth-child(3) h4 {
    color: #E63E68;
}


/* =========================================================
   ORGANIZE IT ALL IN ONE PLACE
========================================================= */

.organize-section {
    width: 100%;

    background: #EAF8F0;

    padding: 20px 6.5% 150px;
}


/* Heading */

.organize-section > h2 {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 60px;
    line-height: 1.15;

    font-weight: 700;

    text-align: center;

    margin-bottom: 80px;
}


/* Grid */

.organize-grid {
    width: 100%;

    display: grid;

    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);

    gap: 70px 45px;
}


/* =========================================================
   ORGANIZE CARDS
========================================================= */

.organize-card {
    position: relative;

    min-height: 225px;

    padding: 55px 40px;

    border-radius: 25px;

    overflow: hidden;

    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: flex-start;
}


/* Card title */

.organize-card h3 {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 30px;
    line-height: 1.2;

    font-weight: 700;

    margin-bottom: 12px;
}


/* Card description */

.organize-card p {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 18px;
    line-height: 1.4;

    max-width: 300px;
}


/* Card icon */

.organize-card > img {
    width: 65px;
    height: 65px;

    object-fit: contain;

    flex-shrink: 0;
}


/* =========================================================
   ORGANIZE CARD COLORS
========================================================= */

.hotels {
    background: #A9D4F9;
}

.hotels p {
    color: #435F82;
}

.car-rental {
    background: #B9F0C0;
}

.car-rental p {
    color: #24583D;
}

.flight {
    background: #FFE990;
}

.flight p {
    color: #625A32;
}

.restaurants {
    background: #E8B17D;
}

.restaurants p {
    color: #804624;
}

.experiences {
    background: #BDA1F2;
}

.experiences p {
    color: #4E3C84;
}

.tours {
    background: #86BFF0;
}

.tours p {
    color: #245A92;
}


/* =========================================================
   FOOTER
========================================================= */

.footer {
    width: 100%;

    background: #EAF8F0;

    padding: 80px 8% 100px;
}


/* Footer columns */

.footer-content {
    width: 100%;

    display: grid;

    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr;

    justify-content: end;

    gap: 80px;

    padding-left: 45%;
}


/* Individual column */

.footer-column {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;

    align-items: center;

    text-align: center;

    gap: 20px;
}


/* Column heading */

.footer-column h3 {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 26px;
    font-weight: 500;

    margin-bottom: 5px;
}


/* "for" text */

.footer-column h3 span {
    font-family: Georgia, serif;

    font-style: italic;

    font-weight: 400;

    margin-right: 5px;
}


/* Footer links */

.footer-column a {
    font-family: "Inter", sans-serif;

    font-size: 14px;
    font-weight: 400;

    transition: opacity 0.2s ease;
}

.footer-column a:hover {
    opacity: 0.6;
}


/* Business heading */

.business-heading {
    margin-top: 35px;
}


/* =========================================================
   SOCIAL LINKS
========================================================= */

.social-links {
    width: 100%;

    display: flex;
    justify-content: flex-end;

    gap: 75px;

    margin-top: 100px;

    padding-right: 3%;
}

.social-links a {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
}

.social-links img {
    width: 34px;
    height: 34px;

    object-fit: contain;
}


/* =========================================================
   RESPONSIVE DESIGN
========================================================= */

@media (max-width: 1200px) {

    .navbar {
        padding: 25px 40px;
    }

    .nav-links {
        gap: 30px;
        margin-right: 35px;
    }

    .hero-text h1 {
        font-size: 100px;
    }

    .hero-text p {
        font-size: 22px;
    }

    .how-it-works > h2,
    .section-heading h2 {
        font-size: 70px;
    }

    .how-text p {
        font-size: 26px;
    }

    .destination-collage {
        transform: scale(0.85);
        transform-origin: center;
    }

    .organize-grid {
        gap: 30px;
    }

    .organize-card {
        padding: 40px 30px;
    }

    .footer-content {
        padding-left: 35%;
    }

}


/* =========================================================
   TABLET
========================================================= */

@media (max-width: 900px) {

    .navbar {
        padding: 20px 30px;
    }

    .nav-links {
        display: none;
    }

    .nav-actions {
        margin-left: auto;
    }

    .hero-text h1 {
        font-size: 78px;
    }

    .hero-text p {
        font-size: 20px;
    }

    .how-section {
        flex-direction: column;

        gap: 50px;

        margin-bottom: 100px;
    }

    .how-text {
        max-width: 100%;
    }

    .destination-collage {
        width: 600px;
        max-width: 100%;
    }

    .feature-cards {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .organize-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
    }

    .footer-content {
        padding-left: 0;
    }

}


/* =========================================================
   MOBILE
========================================================= */

@media (max-width: 600px) {

    .hero {
        min-height: 750px;

        border-radius: 0 0 50px 50px;

        background-position: 65% top;
    }

    .navbar {
        height: 80px;

        padding: 15px 20px;
    }

    .logo {
        width: 85px;
    }

    .menu-button img {
        width: 30px;
    }

    .nav-actions {
        gap: 12px;

        font-size: 15px;
    }

    .get-started-button {
        padding: 10px 15px;
    }

    .hero-content {
        min-height: 750px;

        padding: 120px 25px 50px;

        align-items: flex-start;
    }

    .hero-text h1 {
        font-size: 58px;

        letter-spacing: -2px;
    }

    .hero-text p {
        font-size: 18px;

        line-height: 1.4;
    }

    .hero-buttons {
        gap: 12px;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
    }

    .hero-button {
        font-size: 16px;

        padding: 12px 18px;
    }

    .learn-more {
        font-size: 16px;
        bottom: 25px;
    }


    /* How it works */

    .how-it-works {
        padding: 70px 25px;
    }

    .how-it-works > h2,
    .section-heading h2 {
        font-size: 48px;
    }

    .how-it-works > h2 {
        margin-bottom: 70px;
    }

    .how-text h3 {
        font-size: 30px;
    }

    .how-text p {
        font-size: 20px;
    }


    /* Destination collage */

    .destination-collage {
        height: 400px;

        transform: scale(0.65);
        transform-origin: center;
    }


    /* Features */

    .section-heading {
        margin-bottom: 45px;
    }

    .section-heading p {
        font-size: 23px;
    }

    .feature-cards {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .feature-card h3 {
        font-size: 26px;
    }

    .feature-card h4 {
        font-size: 21px;
    }


    /* Organize */

    .organize-section {
        padding: 30px 20px 80px;
    }

    .organize-section > h2 {
        font-size: 42px;

        margin-bottom: 50px;
    }

    .organize-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;

        gap: 25px;
    }

    .organize-card {
        min-height: 200px;

        padding: 40px 30px;
    }


    /* Footer */

    .footer {
        padding: 60px 25px;
    }

    .footer-content {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;

        padding-left: 0;

        gap: 60px;
    }

    .social-links {
        justify-content: center;

        gap: 30px;

        margin-top: 70px;

        padding-right: 0;
    }

}
```

### Then run it in VS Code

Open `index.html`, then either:

**Option 1 — Live Server**

If you have the **Live Server** extension:

1. Right-click `index.html`
2. Click **Open with Live Server**
3. Your website will open in Chrome.
4. Every time you save HTML/CSS, the browser updates.

If you don't have Live Server, you can simply double-click `index.html` for now.

If your downloaded Figma assets have different names, **the page will show blank spaces where those images should be**.

# 1. HTML — `index.html`

Create a new HTML file and paste this:

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">

    <title>GoMigo - Main Menu</title>

    <!-- Google Fonts -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>

    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=K2D:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Manrope:wght@400;500;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">

    <!-- CSS -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css">
</head>

<body>

<div class="page">

    <!-- =====================================================
         SIDEBAR
    ====================================================== -->

    <aside class="sidebar">

        <!-- Logo -->
        <div class="sidebar-logo">
            <img src="assets/gomigo-logo.png" alt="GoMigo">
        </div>

        <!-- Navigation -->
        <nav class="sidebar-nav">

            <a href="#" class="nav-button active">
                Plan Trip
            </a>

            <a href="#" class="nav-button">
                My Trips
            </a>

            <a href="#" class="nav-button">
                Explore
            </a>

            <a href="#" class="nav-button">
                Saved
            </a>

            <a href="#" class="nav-button">
                Travel Guide
            </a>

        </nav>


        <!-- Side quote -->
        <div class="sidebar-quote">
            Good<br>
            Trips<br>
            Happen<br>
            Here ♡
        </div>


        <!-- Profile -->
        <div class="sidebar-profile">

            <img src="assets/profile.png" alt="Profile">

            <div>
                <h4>Hey, Explorer!</h4>
                <p>Let’s plan something amazing!</p>
            </div>

        </div>

    </aside>


    <!-- =====================================================
         MAIN CONTENT
    ====================================================== -->

    <main class="main-content">


        <!-- =================================================
             HERO
        ================================================== -->

        <section class="hero">

            <img
                src="assets/main-menu-hero.png"
                alt="Travel destination"
                class="hero-image"
            >

            <!-- Hero text -->
            <div class="hero-text">

                <div class="hello-text">
                    Hello there!
                </div>

                <h1>
                    Where’s your<br>
                    next adventure?
                </h1>

                <p>
                    Tell us a little, and we’ll take care of the rest.
                </p>

            </div>


            <!-- Decorative text -->
            <div class="hero-decoration">
                Same<br>
                Planet<br>
                A Kinder Way<br>
                to See It
            </div>

        </section>



        <!-- =================================================
             TRIP PLANNING FORM
        ================================================== -->

        <section class="planning-box">

            <div class="planning-field">

                <h3>Where to?</h3>

                <p>
                    Any place you wish to
                </p>

            </div>


            <div class="planning-field">

                <h3>Travel Dates</h3>

                <p>
                    Add dates
                </p>

            </div>


            <div class="planning-field">

                <h3>Travelers</h3>

                <p>
                    1 Traveler
                </p>

            </div>


            <button class="start-planning">
                Start Planning →
            </button>

        </section>



        <!-- =================================================
             INSPIRATION SECTION
        ================================================== -->

        <section class="inspiration">

            <div class="section-title">

                <h2>
                    Need some inspiration?
                </h2>

                <p>
                    Explore trending destinations or let GoMigo surprise you.
                </p>

            </div>


            <!-- Destination cards -->

            <div class="destination-grid">


                <!-- Greece -->
                <article class="destination-card">

                    <div class="destination-image">

                        <img
                            src="assets/greece.png"
                            alt="Greece"
                        >

                        <button class="heart">
                            ♡
                        </button>

                    </div>

                    <div class="destination-info">

                        <h3>Greece</h3>

                        <p>
                            Islands, culture and sunsets
                        </p>

                    </div>

                </article>


                <!-- Switzerland -->
                <article class="destination-card">

                    <div class="destination-image">

                        <img
                            src="assets/switzerland.png"
                            alt="Switzerland"
                        >

                        <button class="heart">
                            ♡
                        </button>

                    </div>

                    <div class="destination-info">

                        <h3>Switzerland</h3>

                        <p>
                            Mountains, lakes and more
                        </p>

                    </div>

                </article>


                <!-- Japan -->
                <article class="destination-card">

                    <div class="destination-image">

                        <img
                            src="assets/japan.png"
                            alt="Japan"
                        >

                        <button class="heart">
                            ♡
                        </button>

                    </div>

                    <div class="destination-info">

                        <h3>Japan</h3>

                        <p>
                            Tradition meets modern
                        </p>

                    </div>

                </article>


                <!-- Jaipur -->
                <article class="destination-card">

                    <div class="destination-image">

                        <img
                            src="assets/jaipur.png"
                            alt="Jaipur"
                        >

                        <button class="heart">
                            ♡
                        </button>

                    </div>

                    <div class="destination-info">

                        <h3>Jaipur</h3>

                        <p>
                            Royal charm, vibrant streets
                        </p>

                    </div>

                </article>

            </div>

        </section>



        <!-- =================================================
             SURPRISE SECTION
        ================================================== -->

        <section class="surprise-section">

            <img
                src="assets/surprise-background.png"
                alt="Travel destination"
                class="surprise-image"
            >


            <div class="surprise-content">

                <div class="small-heading">
                    NOT SURE WHERE TO GO?
                </div>

                <h2>
                    Let GoMigo<br>
                    Surprise You
                </h2>

                <p>
                    Answer a few quick questions and we’ll recommend<br>
                    the perfect destination for you.
                </p>

                <button class="surprise-button">
                    I’m Open to Ideas →
                </button>

            </div>


            <div class="surprise-decoration">
                Adventure<br>
                Finds You<br>
                Too ♡
            </div>

        </section>


    </main>

</div>


</body>
</html>
```

# 2. CSS — `style.css`

Now create `style.css` in the same folder:

```css
/* =========================================================
   GOMIGO MAIN MENU
========================================================= */

* {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
}

html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
}

body {
    background: #f7f0e8;
    color: #07566b;

    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    overflow-x: hidden;
}

img {
    display: block;
    max-width: 100%;
}

button {
    font-family: inherit;
}


/* =========================================================
   PAGE
========================================================= */

.page {
    width: 100%;
    min-height: 100vh;

    display: flex;
}


/* =========================================================
   SIDEBAR
========================================================= */

.sidebar {
    position: fixed;

    left: 0;
    top: 0;

    width: 145px;
    height: 100vh;

    background: #edf8ef;

    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;

    padding: 28px 15px;

    z-index: 100;
}


/* =========================================================
   LOGO
========================================================= */

.sidebar-logo {
    width: 100%;

    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;

    margin-bottom: 25px;
}

.sidebar-logo img {
    width: 88px;
    height: auto;
}


/* =========================================================
   SIDEBAR NAVIGATION
========================================================= */

.sidebar-nav {
    width: 100%;

    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;

    gap: 17px;
}

.nav-button {
    width: 100%;

    padding: 7px 5px;

    border-radius: 25px;

    background: #b9e1ed;

    color: #23647a;

    text-align: center;

    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;
    font-size: 13px;
    font-weight: 500;

    transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

.nav-button:hover {
    background: #a6d6e4;
}

.nav-button.active {
    background: #b6dfeb;
}


/* =========================================================
   SIDEBAR QUOTE
========================================================= */

.sidebar-quote {
    margin-top: 110px;

    text-align: center;

    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 18px;

    line-height: 1.45;

    font-style: italic;

    color: #246e7e;
}


/* =========================================================
   SIDEBAR PROFILE
========================================================= */

.sidebar-profile {
    margin-top: auto;

    width: 100%;

    padding: 12px 8px;

    background: #e8f8ee;

    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;

    text-align: center;
}

.sidebar-profile img {
    width: 35px;
    height: 35px;

    border-radius: 50%;

    object-fit: cover;

    margin-bottom: 5px;
}

.sidebar-profile h4 {
    font-size: 10px;

    font-weight: 700;

    color: #07566b;
}

.sidebar-profile p {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 7px;

    color: #477585;
}


/* =========================================================
   MAIN CONTENT
========================================================= */

.main-content {
    width: calc(100% - 145px);

    margin-left: 145px;

    padding: 0 28px 60px;

    background: #f7f0e8;
}


/* =========================================================
   HERO
========================================================= */

.hero {
    position: relative;

    width: 100%;

    height: 300px;

    overflow: hidden;

    border-radius: 0 0 0 0;
}

.hero-image {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;

    object-fit: cover;

    object-position: center;
}


/* Hero text */

.hero-text {
    position: absolute;

    left: 9%;

    top: 45px;

    color: #07566b;
}

.hello-text {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 18px;

    font-style: italic;

    margin-left: -15px;

    margin-bottom: 3px;

    transform: rotate(-7deg);
}

.hero-text h1 {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 34px;

    line-height: 1.08;

    font-weight: 600;

    letter-spacing: -1px;

    margin-bottom: 8px;
}

.hero-text p {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 13px;

    font-weight: 500;
}


/* =========================================================
   HERO DECORATION
========================================================= */

.hero-decoration {
    position: absolute;

    right: 20%;

    top: 105px;

    color: #236c7d;

    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 15px;

    line-height: 1.05;

    font-style: italic;

    transform: rotate(-8deg);

    text-align: center;
}


/* =========================================================
   PLANNING BOX
========================================================= */

.planning-box {
    position: relative;

    width: 78%;

    min-height: 68px;

    margin: -34px auto 0;

    background: #ffffff;

    border-radius: 20px;

    display: flex;
    align-items: center;

    padding: 10px;

    box-shadow: 0 5px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);

    z-index: 5;
}


/* Planning fields */

.planning-field {
    flex: 1;

    padding: 0 25px;

    border-right: 1px solid #ddd6cf;
}

.planning-field h3 {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 12px;

    font-weight: 700;

    margin-bottom: 3px;

    color: #24687a;
}

.planning-field p {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 10px;

    color: #b3a79d;
}


/* Start planning */

.start-planning {
    border: none;

    background: #07566b;

    color: #ffffff;

    border-radius: 30px;

    padding: 11px 22px;

    margin-left: 12px;

    font-size: 13px;

    cursor: pointer;

    white-space: nowrap;

    transition: 0.2s ease;
}

.start-planning:hover {
    background: #06485a;
}


/* =========================================================
   INSPIRATION
========================================================= */

.inspiration {
    padding: 18px 2% 0;
}


/* Section heading */

.section-title {
    margin-bottom: 22px;
}

.section-title h2 {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 25px;

    font-weight: 600;

    color: #07566b;

    margin-bottom: 1px;
}

.section-title p {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 12px;

    color: #07566b;
}


/* =========================================================
   DESTINATION GRID
========================================================= */

.destination-grid {
    display: grid;

    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);

    gap: 20px;

    width: 100%;
}


/* =========================================================
   DESTINATION CARD
========================================================= */

.destination-card {
    background: #ffffff;

    border-radius: 15px;

    overflow: hidden;

    box-shadow: 0 3px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03);
}


/* Image */

.destination-image {
    position: relative;

    width: 100%;

    height: 85px;

    overflow: hidden;
}

.destination-image img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;

    object-fit: cover;
}


/* Heart */

.heart {
    position: absolute;

    right: 7px;
    top: 7px;

    width: 21px;
    height: 21px;

    border-radius: 50%;

    border: 1px solid #b9dce5;

    background: #ffffff;

    color: #39829a;

    font-size: 14px;

    line-height: 20px;

    padding: 0;

    cursor: pointer;
}


/* Destination info */

.destination-info {
    padding: 5px 8px 8px;
}

.destination-info h3 {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 10px;

    line-height: 1;

    color: #07566b;

    font-weight: 700;

    margin-bottom: 3px;
}

.destination-info p {
    font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif;

    font-size: 7px;

    line-height: 1.1;

    color: #8c8985;
}


/* =========================================================
   SURPRISE SECTION
========================================================= */

.surprise-section {
    position: relative;

    width: 100%;

    height: 215px;

    margin-top: 28px;

    border-radius: 22px;

    overflow: hidden;
}


/* Background */

.surprise-image {
    position: absolute;

    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;

    object-fit: cover;

    object-position: center;

    inset: 0;
}


/* =========================================================
   SURPRISE CONTENT
========================================================= */

.surprise-content {
    position: absolute;

    left: 3%;

    top: 27px;

    z-index: 2;

    color: #07566b;
}

.small-heading {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 11px;

    font-weight: 600;

    margin-bottom: 3px;
}

.surprise-content h2 {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 28px;

    line-height: 1.02;

    font-weight: 600;

    margin-bottom: 6px;
}

.surprise-content p {
    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 11px;

    line-height: 1.15;

    font-weight: 500;

    margin-bottom: 9px;
}


/* Button */

.surprise-button {
    border: none;

    background: #07566b;

    color: white;

    padding: 8px 15px;

    border-radius: 25px;

    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 12px;

    cursor: pointer;

    transition: 0.2s ease;
}

.surprise-button:hover {
    background: #06485a;
}


/* =========================================================
   SURPRISE DECORATION
========================================================= */

.surprise-decoration {
    position: absolute;

    right: 28%;

    top: 18px;

    z-index: 3;

    font-family: "K2D", sans-serif;

    font-size: 15px;

    line-height: 1.05;

    color: #6c9294;

    font-style: italic;

    text-align: center;

    transform: rotate(-8deg);
}


/* =========================================================
   TABLET
========================================================= */

@media (max-width: 1000px) {

    .sidebar {
        width: 115px;
    }

    .main-content {
        width: calc(100% - 115px);

        margin-left: 115px;
    }

    .destination-grid {
        gap: 12px;
    }

    .planning-box {
        width: 90%;
    }

    .hero-text h1 {
        font-size: 30px;
    }
}


/* =========================================================
   MOBILE
========================================================= */

@media (max-width: 700px) {

    .page {
        display: block;
    }

    /* Sidebar becomes top bar */

    .sidebar {
        position: relative;

        width: 100%;
        height: auto;

        padding: 12px 20px;

        flex-direction: row;

        justify-content: space-between;

        gap: 15px;
    }

    .sidebar-logo {
        width: auto;

        margin: 0;
    }

    .sidebar-logo img {
        width: 75px;
    }

    .sidebar-nav {
        flex-direction: row;

        gap: 5px;

        overflow-x: auto;
    }

    .nav-button {
        min-width: 70px;

        font-size: 9px;

        padding: 6px 8px;
    }

    .sidebar-quote,
    .sidebar-profile {
        display: none;
    }


    /* Main */

    .main-content {
        width: 100%;

        margin-left: 0;

        padding: 0 12px 30px;
    }


    /* Hero */

    .hero {
        height: 300px;
    }

    .hero-text {
        left: 7%;
        top: 45px;
    }

    .hero-text h1 {
        font-size: 27px;
    }

    .hero-text p {
        font-size: 10px;
    }

    .hero-decoration {
        display: none;
    }


    /* Planning */

    .planning-box {
        width: 95%;

        margin-top: -25px;

        flex-wrap: wrap;

        padding: 10px;
    }

    .planning-field {
        padding: 5px 10px;

        min-width: 33%;
    }

    .start-planning {
        width: 100%;

        margin: 8px 0 0;
    }


    /* Destination cards */

    .destination-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
    }


    /* Surprise */

    .surprise-section {
        height: 240px;
    }

    .surprise-decoration {
        display: none;
    }
}
```

---

# 3. Your assets folder

For this page, put the images you downloaded from Figma inside:

```text
GoMigo/
│
├── index.html
├── style.css
│
└── assets/
    │
    ├── gomigo-logo.png
    ├── main-menu-hero.png
    ├── profile.png
    │
    ├── greece.png
    ├── switzerland.png
    ├── japan.png
    ├── jaipur.png
    │
    └── surprise-background.png
```

### Important

The names above are **the names used by my code**. Your actual downloaded Figma files may have names such as:

```text
image 23.png
Rectangle 45.png
Frame 103.png
Asset 7.png
```

That's completely fine. **You don't have to rename everything.** Just change the filename in the HTML.

For example, if your Greece image is:

```text
assets/image 23.png
```

change:

```html
<img src="assets/greece.png" alt="Greece">
```

to:

```html
<img src="assets/image 23.png" alt="Greece">
```

---

## 4. How to run it in VS Code

Your VS Code should look like:

```text
📁 GoMigo
   │
   ├── 📄 index.html
   ├── 📄 style.css
   │
   └── 📁 assets
        ├── 🖼 gomigo-logo.png
        ├── 🖼 main-menu-hero.png
        ├── 🖼 profile.png
        ├── 🖼 greece.png
        ├── 🖼 switzerland.png
        ├── 🖼 japan.png
        ├── 🖼 jaipur.png
        └── 🖼 surprise-background.png
```

Then:

**VS Code → right-click `index.html` → Open with Live Server**

Your browser will open the page.

Since you already downloaded the **exact assets from Figma**, don't use random replacement images. Put your actual Figma assets into `assets/`.

Then **send me a screenshot of your `assets` folder in VS Code**.

I'll map **each of your actual filenames to the correct place in this HTML**, so you won't have to figure out which image is which.
